      139 +### Block 7: A Learned Model Compared with Baselines          
      140 +* **Implementation:** a multinomial Naive Bayes classifier (`s
          +rc/acc_mcp/intent_model.py`) that predicts the tier from the w
          +ords of `task_intent` (Laplace smoothing, log-probabilities, n
          +o new dependencies). It targets the main Block 4 error source,
          + the keyword rule, and is evaluated offline only; the server d
          +oes not use it.                                               
      141 +* **Evaluation:** trained on the Block 4 train split (70 tasks
          +, seed 21) and compared on the same 30 holdout tasks with two 
          +baselines: always the most common train tier (Tier 3), and the
          + keyword rule read directly as a tier.                        
      142 +* **Result:** holdout accuracy is 46.7% for Naive Bayes, 70.0%
          + for the keyword rule and 40.0% for the majority baseline. Nai
          +ve Bayes overfits (98.6% on its own train tasks) and does **no
          +t** beat the keyword rule, which stays in the server. Its most
          + distinctive words (`callers`, `rename`, `fix`, `inspect`, ...
          +) are close to the keyword lists.                             
      143  
      144 +### Block 8: Uncertainty on the Holdout                       
      145 +* **Implementation:** `src/acc_mcp/uncertainty.py` (no new dep
          +endencies): Wilson and seeded bootstrap 95% intervals for accu
          +racy, miss rate and false alarm rate; exact McNemar tests and 
          +paired bootstrap intervals on the same holdout tasks; and a sw
          +eep over 50 stratified splits.                                
      146 +* **Results:**                                                
      147 +  * Every holdout accuracy interval is about 30 points wide (f
          +itted weights: 70.0%, 52.1–83.3%).                            
      148 +  * Fitted vs hand-picked weights: +3.3 points, paired interva
          +l −20.0 to +26.7, McNemar p = 1.0, so fitting did not measurab
          +ly help. It traded misses for false alarms.                   
      149 +  * Naive Bayes vs keyword rule: −23.3 points, paired interval
          + −43.3 to −3.3, McNemar p = 0.065: weak evidence that the keyw
          +ord rule is better.                                           
      150 +  * Over 50 splits, the mean holdout accuracy is 63.7% for the
          + logistic model, 59.4% for Naive Bayes and 69.2% for the keywo
          +rd rule alone. Seed 21 was a favourable split for the logistic
          + model.                                                       
      151 +* **What the data cannot conclude:** there are 30 holdout task
          +s, the labels were written by the project author, and train an
          +d holdout share code snippets. The numbers describe the models
          + on this dataset only, and differences whose paired interval i
          +ncludes 0 are not evidence.                                   
      152 +                                                              
      153 +### Exploratory: Pi Benchmark (not a claimed block)           
      154 +* **What it is:** a bug-injection benchmark (`eval/PROTOCOL.md
          +`, `eval/pi_bench.py`) on pinned releases of more-itertools an
          +d click. It has 10 tasks (6 direct, 4 call-graph), done by han
          +d in interactive Pi, plain vs ACC-MCP, and graded by the libra
          +ries' own tests. It measures navigation calls before the targe
          +t edit, code-reading tokens, wasted and large reads, peak cont
          +ext, and with ACC-MCP the tiers and explicit and silent escala
          +tions.                                                        
      155 +* **What exists:** the pilot (plain, `mi_difference`) and one 
          +trace pair of `mi_split_at`; both trace runs resolved the bug.
      156 +* **Why it is not claimed:** separating ACC-MCP from run-to-ru
          +n noise of an unseeded model needs at least 10 tasks × 3 runs 
          +per mode (about 60 hand runs). The one `mi_split_at` pair diff
          +ers by a factor of 9 in model calls (11 plain, 98 acc), mostly
          + because of one wrong first fix, which shows how large that no
          +ise is.
